- **flutter-perf-build-and-rebuild**: Use when a screen rebuilds too much, a toggle or search input rebuilds a whole page, or build() does computation. Fixes expensive work inside Flutter build() methods and over-broad rebuilds: repeated sorting/filtering/formatting in build, widget-returning helper methods, missing const, builder widgets rebuilding static children, and state subscriptions (Riverpod select, Consumer, scoped providers) that rebuild too much. Do not use for list construction (flutter-perf-lists-and-scrolling) or derived-state caching (flutter-perf-derived-state). ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-build-and-rebuild))
- **flutter-perf-custom-painting**: Use when DevTools shows a CustomPaint or custom RenderObject taking long paint or layout time, or a chart, canvas or drawing screen drops frames. Reviews Flutter custom painting cost: shouldRepaint returning true, repainting through setState instead of a repaint listenable, Paint, Path and TextPainter objects rebuilt on every paint, drawing offscreen content, static drawing redrawn each frame, fragment shader loading, and RenderObject setters that mark layout or paint when nothing changed. Do not use for Opacity, blur or clipping widgets (flutter-perf-rendering-effects) or for rebuild scope. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-custom-painting))
- **flutter-perf-derived-state**: Use when the same values are recomputed on every rebuild or the client repeatedly scans thousands of records. Optimises derived and aggregated state in a Flutter app without changing results: totals, counts, percentages, balances, scores, grouped or sorted collections, and other values computed from large datasets. Covers memoisation, provider-level caching, incremental calculation, and deciding whether aggregation or pagination belongs on a backend instead of the client. Do not use to change calculation rules. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-derived-state))
- **flutter-perf-images-and-assets**: Audits and fixes Flutter image and asset performance: oversized source images, images decoded far larger than displayed, cacheWidth/cacheHeight, resolution-aware asset variants (arbitrary densities, not just 1x/2x/3x), duplicate density variants, and choosing SVG/vector vs raster for logos, icons, illustrations, photos and thumbnails. Use for high memory on image-heavy screens, slow scrolling with images, or large app assets. Do not convert assets just because another format could be smaller. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-images-and-assets))
- **flutter-perf-isolates**: Use when DevTools shows UI-thread frames blocked by Dart computation. Decides whether and how to move CPU-heavy Flutter work off the main isolate using Isolate.run or compute: large CSV parsing, bulk categorisation, pattern or recurrence detection, big JSON parsing, image manipulation, large local analytics. Do not use for ordinary network or async I/O, or for small computations where isolate overhead exceeds the work. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-isolates))
- **flutter-perf-lists-and-scrolling**: Use for janky scroll, slow first paint of long lists, or thousands of widgets built at once. Improves scrolling and list performance in Flutter: replaces eager ListView(children: data.map(...).toList()) with ListView.builder, GridView.builder or sliver builders for large item histories, and evaluates itemExtent, prototypeItem and itemExtentBuilder to cut layout work. Do not use for small fixed lists, and not for per-item rebuild scope (flutter-perf-build- and-rebuild). ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-lists-and-scrolling))
- **flutter-perf-memory-and-leaks**: Use when memory climbs during normal use, the app is killed on low-memory devices, or a screen keeps working after it was closed. Investigates and fixes Flutter memory growth and leaks: undisposed controllers, focus nodes, timers and stream subscriptions, listeners never removed, BuildContext captured by async work or long-lived objects, unbounded image or data caches, and state that outlives its screen. Uses DevTools memory snapshots and leak tracking. Do not use for slow frames (flutter-perf-build-and-rebuild) or image decode size (flutter-perf-images-and-assets). ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-memory-and-leaks))
- **flutter-perf-profiling-and-validation**: Use when capturing a baseline before optimising, confirming an improvement after, or whenever a performance claim needs evidence. Measures and validates Flutter performance work: flutter run --profile on physical devices, DevTools frame timings (UI vs raster), rebuild tracking, memory and oversized-image checks, realistic large datasets, flutter analyze and flutter test, calculation regression comparison, and backend migration checks when models change. Do not use to implement fixes, and never judge performance from debug mode. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-profiling-and-validation))
- **flutter-perf-rendering-effects**: Use when DevTools shows high raster time or janky raster frames. Optimises only where profiling or implementation evidence shows real cost, and keeps the visual design materially unchanged. Investigates expensive Flutter visual effects and raster cost: Opacity, BackdropFilter, blur, clipping (ClipRRect, ClipPath), large or layered shadows, overlapping transparency, saveLayer, and continuously animated effects. Do not use for UI-thread rebuild problems (flutter-perf-build-and-rebuild). ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-rendering-effects))
- **flutter-perf-startup-and-data-loading**: Use when the app is slow to show its first frame, a screen waits on a chain of requests, a live feed floods the UI, or search fires a request per keystroke. ([KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser](https://github.com/KinwiseNZ-DevOps/flutter-optimiser/tree/HEAD/skills/flutter-perf-startup-and-data-loading))
